// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
    
    return valid;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

--- results ---
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The dominant dependency is the outer “try every remaining seed” loop: each seed’s candidate cluster is independent for a clustering round, while the winning seed must still be selected in original order for deterministic semantics. I’ll parallelize that loop with per-seed results and preserve ordered tie-breaking, and I’ll also remove redundant rescanning inside candidate construction by maintaining each candidate’s current maximum distance incrementally.

+# Link math library
+target_link_libraries(qtclustering PRIVATE OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX m)
--- /dev/fd/63	2026-08-05 12:33:37.969184099 +0200
+++ qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp	2026-08-05 12:33:34.027212083 +0200

+// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
+// 
+// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
+// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
+// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.
+
+#include <algorithm>
+#include <chrono>
+#include <cmath>
+#include <cstdio>
+#include <cstdlib>
+#include <cstring>
+#include <limits>
+#include <omp.h>
+#include <vector>
+
+#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
+
+static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
+static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
+
+// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
+struct Point {
+    double x, y;
+};
+
+// Structure to represent a cluster
+struct Cluster {
+    std::vector<int> members;
+    int seed_point;
+};
+
+// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
+void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
+    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
+    
+    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
+    int count = 0;
+    
+    while (count < N) {
+        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
+        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
+        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
+        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
+        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
+        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
+        
+        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
+        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
+            group_cnt = N - count;
+        }
+        
+        while (group_cnt > 0) {
+            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
+            const double r = frand() * R;
+            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
+            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
+            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
+            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
+            
+            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            
+            points[count] = {x, y};
+            count++;
+            group_cnt--;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
+inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
+    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
+// Find the closest candidate to the current cluster.  max_distances contains
+// the maximum distance from each candidate to the current cluster.  The
+// candidate vector is kept in point-index order so strict comparisons retain
+// the original tie-breaking behavior.
+int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& candidates,
+                     const std::vector<double>& max_distances,
+                     const double threshold) {
+    int closest_point = -1;
+    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
+    
+    for (const int candidate : candidates) {
+        const double max_dist = max_distances[candidate];
+
+        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better
+        // than the current best, retain the first candidate on ties.
+        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
+            min_diameter = max_dist;
+            closest_point = candidate;
+        }
+    }
+    
+    return closest_point;
+}
+
+// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
+// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
+int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
+                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
+                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                              const double threshold,
+                              const int point_count,
+                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
+    std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(point_count, 0);
+    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);
+    std::vector<int> candidates;
+    candidates.reserve(point_count);
+    std::vector<int> members;
+    members.reserve(point_count);
+    
+    // Add seed point
+    in_cluster[seed_point] = 1;
+    members.push_back(seed_point);
+
+    // Initialize the distance from every possible candidate to the seed.
+    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
+    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
+    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+        max_distances[candidate] = distance(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
+        if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+            candidates.push_back(candidate);
+        }
+    }
+    
+    // Iteratively add closest points
+    while (!candidates.empty()) {
+        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
+        const int closest = findClosestPoint(candidates, max_distances, threshold);
+        
+        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
+        
+        in_cluster[closest] = 1;
+        members.push_back(closest);
+
+        // Update all remaining candidates with their distance to the newly
+        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
+        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
+        // candidate on every iteration.
+        const Point& new_member = points[closest];
+        size_t write = 0;
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
+            const int candidate = candidates[read];
+            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
+                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));
+
+            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
+            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
+            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+                candidates[write++] = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        candidates.resize(write);
+    }
+    
+    // Copy members if requested
+    if (cluster_members) {
+        *cluster_members = members;
+    }
+    
+    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
+}
+
+// Main QT clustering algorithm
+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  const double threshold) {
+    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
+    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
+    clusters.reserve(N);
+    
+    // Initialize unclustered indices
+    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
+        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
+    }
+    
+    // Main clustering loop
+    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
+        int max_cardinality = -1;
+        int best_seed = -1;
+        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
+        
+        // Candidate clusters are independent for a given clustering round.
+        // Store only their cardinalities in parallel; selecting the winner
+        // afterward in index order preserves the sequential tie-breaking
+        // semantics.  The winning cluster is regenerated once below.
+        const int seed_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
+        std::vector<int> candidate_cardinalities(seed_count);
+
+        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
+        for (int i = 0; i < seed_count; ++i) {
+            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+            candidate_cardinalities[i] = clustered[seed]
+                ? 0
+                : generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,
+                                           threshold, N);
+        }
+
+        for (int i = 0; i < seed_count; ++i) {
+            const int cardinality = candidate_cardinalities[i];
+            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
+                max_cardinality = cardinality;
+                best_seed = unclustered_indices[i];
+            }
+        }
+        
+        // If we found a cluster, add it
+        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
+                                     &best_cluster_members);
+
+            Cluster cluster;
+            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
+            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
+            clusters.push_back(cluster);
+            
+            // Mark all members as clustered
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
+                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = 1;
+            }
+            
+            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
+            unclustered_indices.erase(
+                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
+                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
+                unclustered_indices.end()
+            );
+        } else {
+            // No more clusters can be formed
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    
+    return clusters;
+}
+
+// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
+bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
+                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                     const double threshold) {
+    bool valid = true;
+    
+    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
+    
+    // Check each cluster
+    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
+        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
+        double max_diameter = 0.0;
+        
+        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
+            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
+                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
+                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
+                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
+            }
+        }
+        
+        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
+            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
+                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
+        }
+        
+        // Validate diameter is within threshold
+        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
+            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
+                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
+            valid = false;
+        }
+    }
+    
+    // Check for duplicate memberships
+    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
+    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
+            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
+            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
+                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
+                       member, membership[member], c);
+                valid = false;
+            }
+            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
+        }
+    }
+    
+    // Count clustered points
+    int clustered_count = 0;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
+        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
+    }
+    
+    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
+           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
+    
+    return valid;
+}
+
+void printUsage(const char* progName) {
+    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
+    printf("Options:\n");
+    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
+    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
+    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
+}
+
+int main(int argc, char** argv) {
+    int num_points = 1000;
+    double threshold = 2.0;
+    bool validate = false;
+    bool printResults = false;
+    
+    // Parse command line arguments
+    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
+        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
+        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
+            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
+        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
+            validate = true;
+        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
+            printResults = true;
+        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
+            printUsage(argv[0]);
+            return 0;
+        } else {
+            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
+            printUsage(argv[0]);
+            return 1;
+        }
+    }
+    
+    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
+        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
+               num_points, threshold);
+        return 1;
+    }
+    
+    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
+    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
+    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
+    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+    
+    // Generate synthetic data
+    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
+    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
+    
+    // Perform QT clustering
+    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
+    
+    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
+    
+    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
+    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
+        cluster_end - cluster_start);
+    
+    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
+    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
+    
+    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
+    int total_clustered = 0;
+    int max_cluster_size = 0;
+    
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
+        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
+        total_clustered += size;
+        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
+    }
+    
+    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
+        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
+    
+    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
+           total_clustered, num_points, 
+           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
+    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
+    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
+    
+    // Performance metrics
+    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
+    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
+    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
+    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
+           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
+    
+    // Print results for external validation
+    if (printResults) {
+        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
+        std::vector<double> membershipData;
+        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
+        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
+        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
+            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
+                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
+            }
+        }
+        for (int m : membership) {
+            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
+        }
+        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
+    }
+    
+    // Validation
+    if (validate) {
+        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
+        
+        if (valid) {
+            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            return 0;
+        } else {
+            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+            return 1;
+        }
+    }
+    
+    return 0;
+}
// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <omp.h>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest candidate to the current cluster.  max_distances contains
// the maximum distance from each candidate to the current cluster.  The
// candidate vector is kept in point-index order so strict comparisons retain
// the original tie-breaking behavior.
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& candidates,
                     const std::vector<double>& max_distances,
                     const double threshold) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    for (const int candidate : candidates) {
        const double max_dist = max_distances[candidate];

        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better
        // than the current best, retain the first candidate on ties.
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(point_count, 0);
    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);
    std::vector<int> candidates;
    candidates.reserve(point_count);
    std::vector<int> members;
    members.reserve(point_count);
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = 1;
    members.push_back(seed_point);

    // Initialize the distance from every possible candidate to the seed.
    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;

        max_distances[candidate] = distance(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
        if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
            candidates.push_back(candidate);
        }
    }
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (!candidates.empty()) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(candidates, max_distances, threshold);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = 1;
        members.push_back(closest);

        // Update all remaining candidates with their distance to the newly
        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
        // candidate on every iteration.
        const Point& new_member = points[closest];
        size_t write = 0;
        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
            const int candidate = candidates[read];
            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));

            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
                candidates[write++] = candidate;
            }
        }
        candidates.resize(write);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
    clusters.reserve(N);
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Candidate clusters are independent for a given clustering round.
        // Store only their cardinalities in parallel; selecting the winner
        // afterward in index order preserves the sequential tie-breaking
        // semantics.  The winning cluster is regenerated once below.
        const int seed_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
        std::vector<int> candidate_cardinalities(seed_count);

        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
        for (int i = 0; i < seed_count; ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            candidate_cardinalities[i] = clustered[seed]
                ? 0
                : generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,
                                           threshold, N);
        }

        for (int i = 0; i < seed_count; ++i) {
            const int cardinality = candidate_cardinalities[i];
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
                                     &best_cluster_members);

            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = 1;
            }

-// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold

 // Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
 // Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
 int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
-                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
+                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
                               const std::vector<Point>& points,

                               const int point_count,

-    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(point_count, 0);
+    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);
+    std::vector<int> candidates;
+    candidates.reserve(point_count);
     std::vector<int> members;
+    members.reserve(point_count);
     
     // Add seed point
-    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
+    in_cluster[seed_point] = 1;
     members.push_back(seed_point);
+
+    // Initialize the distance from every possible candidate to the seed.
+    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
+    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
+    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+        max_distances[candidate] = distance(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
+        if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {

+        }

     // Iteratively add closest points
-    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {

-        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
-                                             threshold, point_count);
+        const int closest = findClosestPoint(candidates, max_distances, threshold);

-        in_cluster[closest] = true;
+        in_cluster[closest] = 1;
         members.push_back(closest);
+

+        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
+        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
+        // candidate on every iteration.
+        const Point& new_member = points[closest];
+        size_t write = 0;
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
+            const int candidate = candidates[read];
+            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
+                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));
+
+            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
+            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
+            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+                candidates[write++] = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        candidates.resize(write);
     }

codex
The first OpenMP build exposed a logic bug in the optimized candidate update: the selected candidate must be removed from the active list before updating remaining candidates. I’m correcting that now, then I’ll rerun the baseline-output comparison and timing tests.
apply patch
patch: completed

 // Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
 // Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
 int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
-                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
+                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
                               const std::vector<Point>& points,
                               const double threshold,
                               const int point_count,

-    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> in_cluster(point_count, 0);
+    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);
+    std::vector<int> candidates;
+    candidates.reserve(point_count);
     std::vector<int> members;
+    members.reserve(point_count);
     
     // Add seed point
-    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
+    in_cluster[seed_point] = 1;
     members.push_back(seed_point);
+
+    // Initialize the distance from every possible candidate to the seed.
+    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
+    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
+    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+        max_distances[candidate] = distance(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
+        if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+            candidates.push_back(candidate);

-        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
-                                             threshold, point_count);
+        const int closest = findClosestPoint(candidates, max_distances, threshold);

-        in_cluster[closest] = true;
+        in_cluster[closest] = 1;
         members.push_back(closest);
+

+        // The vector is kept sorted, so this also preserves scan order.
+        candidates.erase(std::lower_bound(candidates.begin(), candidates.end(), closest));
+
+        // Update all remaining candidates with their distance to the newly
+        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
+        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
+        // candidate on every iteration.
+        const Point& new_member = points[closest];
+        size_t write = 0;
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
+            const int candidate = candidates[read];
+            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
+                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));
+
+            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
+            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
+            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+                candidates[write++] = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        candidates.resize(write);
     }

+        candidates.erase(std::lower_bound(candidates.begin(), candidates.end(), closest));

+            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {

 // Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
 // Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
 int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
-                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
+                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
                               const std::vector<Point>& points,
                               const double threshold,
                               const int point_count,
                               std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
-    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
+    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);
+    std::vector<int> candidates;
+    candidates.reserve(point_count);
     std::vector<int> members;
+    members.reserve(point_count);
     
     // Add seed point
-    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
     members.push_back(seed_point);
+
+    // Initialize the distance from every possible candidate to the seed.
+    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
+    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
+    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed_point) continue;
+
+        max_distances[candidate] = distance(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
+        if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+            candidates.push_back(candidate);
+        }
+    }
     
     // Iteratively add closest points
-    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
+    while (!candidates.empty()) {

-        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
-                                             threshold, point_count);
+        size_t closest_position = 0;
+        const int closest = findClosestPoint(candidates, max_distances, threshold,
+                                             closest_position);
         
         if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
         
-        in_cluster[closest] = true;
         members.push_back(closest);
+
+        // Update all remaining candidates with their distance to the newly
+        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
+        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
+        // candidate on every iteration.
+        const Point& new_member = points[closest];
+        size_t write = 0;
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
+            if (read == closest_position) continue;
+
+            const int candidate = candidates[read];
+            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
+                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));
+
+            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
+            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
+            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+                candidates[write++] = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        candidates.resize(write);
     }

 // Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
 // Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
 int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
-                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
+                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,

                               const int point_count,

-    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
+    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);

+    candidates.reserve(point_count);

+    members.reserve(point_count);

-    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;

+    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
+    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
+    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed_point) continue;

-                                             threshold, point_count);

+                                             closest_position);

-        in_cluster[closest] = true;

+        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
+        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
+        // candidate on every iteration.

+        size_t write = 0;
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
+            if (read == closest_position) continue;
+
+            const int candidate = candidates[read];
+            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
+                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));

+            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
+            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
+            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+                candidates[write++] = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        candidates.resize(write);
     }

     1	// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
     2	// 
     3	// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
     4	// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
     5	// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.
     6	
     7	#include <algorithm>
     8	#include <chrono>
     9	#include <cmath>
    10	#include <cstdio>
    11	#include <cstdlib>
    12	#include <cstring>
    13	#include <limits>
    14	#include <omp.h>
    15	#include <vector>
    16	
    17	#include "../common/results_output.hpp"
    18	
    19	static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
    20	static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;
    21	
    22	// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
    23	struct Point {
    24	    double x, y;
    25	};
    26	
    27	// Structure to represent a cluster
    28	struct Cluster {
    29	    std::vector<int> members;
    30	    int seed_point;
    31	};
    32	
    33	// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
    34	void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    35	    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    36	    
    37	    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    38	    int count = 0;
    39	    
    40	    while (count < N) {
    41	        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
    42	        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
    43	        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
    44	        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
    45	        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
    46	        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
    47	        
    48	        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
    49	        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
    50	            group_cnt = N - count;
    51	        }
    52	        
    53	        while (group_cnt > 0) {
    54	            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
    55	            const double r = frand() * R;
    56	            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
    57	            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
    58	            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
    59	            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
    60	            
    61	            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    62	                continue;
    63	            }
    64	            
    65	            points[count] = {x, y};
    66	            count++;
    67	            group_cnt--;
    68	        }
    69	    }
    70	}
    71	
    72	// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
    73	inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    74	    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    75	    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    76	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
    77	}
    78	
    79	// Find the closest candidate to the current cluster.  max_distances contains
    80	// the maximum distance from each candidate to the current cluster.  The
    81	// candidate vector is kept in point-index order so strict comparisons retain
    82	// the original tie-breaking behavior.
    83	int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& candidates,
    84	                     const std::vector<double>& max_distances,
    85	                     const double threshold,
    86	                     size_t& closest_position) {
    87	    int closest_point = -1;
    88	    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    89	    
    90	    for (size_t i = 0; i < candidates.size(); ++i) {
    91	        const int candidate = candidates[i];
    92	        const double max_dist = max_distances[candidate];
    93	
    94	        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better
    95	        // than the current best, retain the first candidate on ties.
    96	        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
    97	            min_diameter = max_dist;
    98	            closest_point = candidate;
    99	            closest_position = i;
   100	        }
   101	    }
   102	    
   103	    return closest_point;
   104	}
   105	
   106	// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
   107	// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
   108	int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
   109	                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
   110	                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
   111	                              const double threshold,
   112	                              const int point_count,
   113	                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
   114	    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);
   115	    std::vector<int> candidates;
   116	    candidates.reserve(point_count);
   117	    std::vector<int> members;
   118	    members.reserve(point_count);
   119	    
   120	    // Add seed point
   121	    members.push_back(seed_point);
   122	
   123	    // Initialize the distance from every possible candidate to the seed.
   124	    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
   125	    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
   126	    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
   127	        if (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed_point) continue;
   128	
   129	        max_distances[candidate] = distance(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
   130	        if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
   131	            candidates.push_back(candidate);
   132	        }
   133	    }
   134	    
   135	    // Iteratively add closest points
   136	    while (!candidates.empty()) {
   137	        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
   138	        size_t closest_position = 0;
   139	        const int closest = findClosestPoint(candidates, max_distances, threshold,
   140	                                             closest_position);
   141	        
   142	        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
   143	        
   144	        members.push_back(closest);
   145	
   146	        // Update all remaining candidates with their distance to the newly
   147	        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
   148	        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
   149	        // candidate on every iteration.
   150	        const Point& new_member = points[closest];
   151	        size_t write = 0;
   152	        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
   153	            if (read == closest_position) continue;
   154	
   155	            const int candidate = candidates[read];
   156	            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
   157	                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));
   158	
   159	            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
   160	            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
   161	            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
   162	                candidates[write++] = candidate;
   163	            }
   164	        }
   165	        candidates.resize(write);
   166	    }
   167	    
   168	    // Copy members if requested
   169	    if (cluster_members) {
   170	        *cluster_members = members;
   171	    }
   172	    
   173	    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
   174	}
   175	
   176	// Main QT clustering algorithm
   177	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   178	                                  const double threshold) {
   179	    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   180	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(N, 0);
   181	    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
   182	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   183	    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);
   184	    clusters.reserve(N);
   185	    
   186	    // Initialize unclustered indices
   187	    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
   188	        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
   189	    }
   190	    
   191	    // Main clustering loop
   192	    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
   193	        int max_cardinality = -1;
   194	        int best_seed = -1;
   195	        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
   196	        
   197	        // Candidate clusters are independent for a given clustering round.
   198	        // Store only their cardinalities in parallel; selecting the winner
   199	        // afterward in index order preserves the sequential tie-breaking
   200	        // semantics.  The winning cluster is regenerated once below.
   201	        const int seed_count = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());
   202	        std::vector<int> candidate_cardinalities(seed_count);
   203	
   204	        #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 1)
   205	        for (int i = 0; i < seed_count; ++i) {
   206	            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
   207	            candidate_cardinalities[i] = clustered[seed]
   208	                ? 0
   209	                : generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,
   210	                                           threshold, N);
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        for (int i = 0; i < seed_count; ++i) {
   214	            const int cardinality = candidate_cardinalities[i];
   215	            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
   216	                max_cardinality = cardinality;
   217	                best_seed = unclustered_indices[i];
   218	            }
   219	        }
   220	        
   221	        // If we found a cluster, add it
   222	        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
   223	            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,
   224	                                     &best_cluster_members);
   225	
   226	            Cluster cluster;
   227	            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
   228	            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
   229	            clusters.push_back(cluster);
   230	            
   231	            // Mark all members as clustered
   232	            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
   233	                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = 1;
   234	            }
   235	            
   236	            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
   237	            unclustered_indices.erase(
   238	                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
   239	                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
   240	                unclustered_indices.end()
   241	            );
   242	        } else {
   243	            // No more clusters can be formed
   244	            break;
   245	        }
   246	    }
   247	    
   248	    return clusters;
   249	}

+// Find the closest candidate to the current cluster.  max_distances contains
+// the maximum distance from each candidate to the current cluster.  The
+// candidate vector is kept in point-index order so strict comparisons retain
+// the original tie-breaking behavior.
+int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& candidates,

-                     const int point_count) {
+                     size_t& closest_position) {

-    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {

-        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
-

-        double max_dist = 0.0;
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-            const int member = cluster_members[i];
-            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
-            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
-        }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < candidates.size(); ++i) {
+        const int candidate = candidates[i];
+        const double max_dist = max_distances[candidate];
+
+        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better
+        // than the current best, retain the first candidate on ties.
         if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
             min_diameter = max_dist;
             closest_point = candidate;
+            closest_position = i;
         }
     }

 // Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
 // Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
 int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
-                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
+                              const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
                               const std::vector<Point>& points,
                               const double threshold,
                               const int point_count,
                               std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
-    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
+    std::vector<double> max_distances(point_count, 0.0);
+    std::vector<int> candidates;
+    candidates.reserve(point_count);
     std::vector<int> members;
+    members.reserve(point_count);
     
     // Add seed point
-    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
     members.push_back(seed_point);
+
+    // Initialize the distance from every possible candidate to the seed.
+    // Candidates that already violate the threshold can never become valid:
+    // adding more members only increases their maximum distance.
+    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
+        if (clustered[candidate] || candidate == seed_point) continue;
+
+        max_distances[candidate] = distance(points[candidate], points[seed_point]);
+        if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+            candidates.push_back(candidate);
+        }
+    }
     
     // Iteratively add closest points
-    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
+    while (!candidates.empty()) {
         // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
-        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
-                                             threshold, point_count);
+        size_t closest_position = 0;
+        const int closest = findClosestPoint(candidates, max_distances, threshold,
+                                             closest_position);
         
         if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
         
-        in_cluster[closest] = true;
         members.push_back(closest);
+
+        // Update all remaining candidates with their distance to the newly
+        // added member.  This is equivalent to recomputing the maximum over
+        // all members, but avoids rescanning the existing cluster for every
+        // candidate on every iteration.
+        const Point& new_member = points[closest];
+        size_t write = 0;
+        for (size_t read = 0; read < candidates.size(); ++read) {
+            if (read == closest_position) continue;
+
+            const int candidate = candidates[read];
+            max_distances[candidate] = std::max(
+                max_distances[candidate], distance(points[candidate], new_member));
+
+            // Once a candidate reaches the threshold it can never be added,
+            // so remove it while preserving candidate-index order.
+            if (max_distances[candidate] < threshold) {
+                candidates[write++] = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+        candidates.resize(write);
     }
     
     // Copy members if requested

-                best_seed = seed;
-                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
+                best_seed = unclustered_indices[i];
             }
         }
         
         // If we found a cluster, add it
         if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
+            generateCandidateCluster(best_seed, clustered, points, threshold, N,

             Cluster cluster;
